### Bài 1 · Niche tourism

Chọn từ a, b, c hoặc d để điền vào chỗ trống.

The idea of niche tourism has become more and more popular, mainly as a reaction to (1) ______ tourism. Niche tourism (2) ______ for small groups of people — perhaps no more than a dozen at a time — who have a shared special interest, e.g. photography, steam engines, bird-watching or gastronomy. Niche tourism provides the opposite of the 'one-size-fits-all' strategy by treating their clientele as (3) ______ and offering them a personal level of service. Niche tourism products are (4) ______ to the client's individual needs and interests.

Because niche tourism is small-scale, it offers an excellent opportunity for people who want to (5) ______ small independent businesses. Tourism is a very (6) ______ business and large tour operators depend on creating economies of (7) ______ in order to be profitable. Small travel companies have been able to (8) ______ areas of business that the major tour operators aren't able to take advantage of.

| | a | b | c | d |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | big | mass | majority | scale |
| 2 | gives | sells | caters | creates |
| 3 | singles | ones | specials | individuals |
| 4 | tailored | suited | provided | made |
| 5 | put up | make up | set up | take up |
| 6 | competing | completion | competitor | competitive |
| 7 | size | scale | place | cost |
| 8 | exploit | use | pay | profit |

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 1</summary>

1 b **mass** · 2 c **caters** · 3 d **individuals** · 4 a **tailored** · 5 c **set up** · 6 d **competitive** · 7 b **scale** · 8 a **exploit**

**Điểm cần nhớ**

- **mass tourism** ↔ **niche tourism** — cặp đối lập trung tâm của Unit 6. *mass* là tính từ ở đây, không phải *big*.
- **cater for somebody** = phục vụ, đáp ứng nhu cầu của ai (Anh); bản Mỹ dùng *cater to*. Cả câu: *Niche tourism caters for small groups.*
- **tailored to** = may đo theo. Đi với *to*, không phải *for*. Cụm cùng họ: *tailor a programme*, *a tailor-made itinerary*.
- **set up a business** = lập doanh nghiệp. *put up* là dựng lên / cho ở nhờ, *take up* là bắt đầu một sở thích — sai nghĩa hẳn.
- **competitive** là tính từ (*a competitive business*); *competing* là phân từ, *competitor* là người, *completion* là "sự hoàn thành" — bẫy đánh vào chỗ nhìn giống nhau.
- **economies of scale** = lợi thế nhờ quy mô. Cụm cố định, luôn số nhiều *economies*.
- **exploit an area of business** = khai thác một khoảng thị trường. Ở đây *exploit* mang nghĩa trung tính, không phải "bóc lột".

</details>

### Bài 2 · Đọc số và thống kê

Viết ra cách bạn sẽ **đọc thành lời** các con số sau.

1. $492,000
2. €6.25m
3. ¥72,525
4. ⅔ *(bản quét không rõ phân số gốc — cứ luyện cả ½ ⅓ ⅔ ¾)*
5. R13.25bn
6. 18–25
7. 3.75%
8. £350,000

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 2</summary>

1. four hundred and ninety-two thousand dollars
2. six point two five million euros
3. seventy-two thousand, five hundred and twenty-five yen
4. two thirds *(½ a half · ⅓ a third · ¾ three quarters)*
5. thirteen point two five billion rand
6. eighteen to twenty-five
7. three point seven five percent
8. three hundred and fifty thousand pounds

**Điểm cần nhớ — luật đọc số trong tiếng Anh nghề**

- **and** trước hàng chục cuối là bắt buộc trong tiếng Anh Anh: *four hundred **and** ninety-two*. Tiếng Anh Mỹ thường bỏ.
- **Phần thập phân đọc từng chữ số**, không đọc thành số: `6.25` là *six point two five*, **không** phải *six point twenty-five*. `3.75%` là *three point seven five percent*.
- **hundred, thousand, million, billion** **không thêm *s*** khi có số đứng trước: *six million euros*, không phải *six millions*. Chỉ thêm *s* khi nói ước lượng: *millions of tourists*.
- **Đơn vị tiền tệ** ở cuối: *492,000 dollars*, *£350,000 = three hundred and fifty thousand pounds*. Ký hiệu đứng trước khi viết, tên tiền đứng sau khi nói.
- **Khoảng** đọc bằng *to*: `18–25` là *eighteen to twenty-five*. Nếu là "từ… đến…" thì *from eighteen to twenty-five*.
- **Phân số:** tử số đọc như số thường, mẫu số đọc như số thứ tự, tử > 1 thì mẫu thêm *s*: *two thirds*, *three quarters*.
- **Tên tiền cần biết:** rand (Nam Phi) · yen (Nhật) · yuan (Trung Quốc) · dirham (UAE) · real (Brazil).

</details>

### Bài 3 · Từ đồng nghĩa dễ hơn

Ghép từ 1–6 với từ đơn giản, thông dụng hơn a–f.

| | | | |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | construct | a | take out |
| 2 | preserve | b | say |
| 3 | extract | c | give |
| 4 | seal | d | build |
| 5 | recite | e | close |
| 6 | grant | f | keep |

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 3</summary>

1–d construct = build · 2–f preserve = keep · 3–a extract = take out · 4–e seal = close · 5–b recite = say · 6–c grant = give

**Điểm cần nhớ — chọn từ theo người nghe**

Cột trái là từ gốc Latin, cột phải là từ gốc Anh–Saxon. Đây không phải chuyện từ nào "đúng hơn", mà là **chọn theo tình huống**:

- **Nói với khách, thuyết minh tại điểm, audio guide** → dùng cột phải. Câu ngắn, từ thông dụng, khách nước ngoài trình độ nào cũng hiểu: *They **built** it in 1732.*
- **Viết report, proposal, press release** → dùng cột trái. Trang trọng và súc tích hơn: *The temple was **constructed** in 1732.*
- Mục [Writing Bank](#writing-bank) nói rõ: bản thuyết minh audio phải viết bằng "relatively simple language", còn proposal phải "formal and neutral". Cùng một nội dung, hai bộ từ khác nhau.

</details>

### Bài 4 · Culture quiz — thể bị động

Hoàn thành các câu bằng động từ ở hộp A và người ở hộp B, dùng **thể bị động** kèm *by*, *for* hoặc *during*.

**Hộp A:** build · compose · paint · write · sing

**Hộp B:** Giuseppe Verdi · JRR Tolkien · Leonardo da Vinci · Michael Jackson · The Ming Dynasty · the pharaohs · William Shakespeare

1. *Imagine* **was composed and sung by** John Lennon. *(ví dụ đã làm mẫu)*
2. *Romeo and Juliet* ______
3. *The Lord of the Rings* ______
4. *The Great Wall of China* ______
5. *Thriller* ______
6. *Rigoletto* ______
7. *The Egyptian Pyramids* ______
8. *The Mona Lisa* ______

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 4</summary>

2. *Romeo and Juliet* **was written by** William Shakespeare.
3. *The Lord of the Rings* **was written by** JRR Tolkien.
4. *The Great Wall of China* **was built during** the Ming Dynasty.
5. *Thriller* **was sung by** Michael Jackson.
6. *Rigoletto* **was composed by** Giuseppe Verdi.
7. *The Egyptian Pyramids* **were built for** the pharaohs.
8. *The Mona Lisa* **was painted by** Leonardo da Vinci.

**Điểm cần nhớ — ba giới từ, ba nghĩa khác nhau**

- **by** + **người làm ra nó**: *painted by Leonardo*, *written by Shakespeare*. Đây là mặc định.
- **for** + **người nó được làm ra để phục vụ**: *built for the pharaohs*. Các pharaoh không tự xây kim tự tháp.
- **during** + **giai đoạn thời gian**: *built during the Ming Dynasty*. Nhà Minh là một triều đại, không phải một người thợ.

Chú ý **were** ở câu 7 vì *The Egyptian Pyramids* số nhiều. Động từ *to be* phải hợp với chủ ngữ mới, không phải với tác nhân.

Vì sao bài này quan trọng với hướng dẫn viên: khi thuyết minh, **thứ đáng nói là hiện vật, không phải người làm**, nên bị động là dạng câu mặc định. So sánh *Leonardo da Vinci painted the 'Mona Lisa'* (câu nói về hoạ sĩ) với *The 'Mona Lisa' was painted by Leonardo da Vinci* (câu nói về bức tranh — đứng trước bức tranh thì nói câu này).

</details>

### Bài 5 · Từ dễ lẫn

Điền câu bằng từ đúng trong hộp. Mỗi từ dùng một lần.

**Hộp từ:** damage · damages · economic · economical · economics · economies · effective · efficient · raise · rise

1. In the Antarctic, temperatures rarely ______ above freezing.
2. Tourism offers excellent potential for the ______ development of emerging countries.
3. The riots and repression could ______ the reputation of the place as a peaceful destination.
4. We need to make ______ by eliminating waste and cutting expenditure.
5. We offer good-quality package tours at ______ prices that everyone can afford.
6. The court awarded her ______ because she did not get the accommodation which she was promised in the brochure.
7. The course focuses on all the aspects of the ______ of tourism — markets, prices, financial investment and growth.
8. The director intends to ______ the museum's profile with the new international exhibition.
9. I have a really ______ filing system. I can find any document almost immediately.
10. How do you think web advertising is ______ in getting new customers?

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 5</summary>

1 rise · 2 economic · 3 damage · 4 economies · 5 economical · 6 damages · 7 economics · 8 raise · 9 efficient · 10 effective

**Điểm cần nhớ — bốn cặp bẫy**

- **rise** (tự tăng, **không** có tân ngữ) ↔ **raise** (làm cho cái gì tăng, **luôn** có tân ngữ). *Temperatures rise.* · *They raise the museum's profile.* ❌ *raise above freezing* ❌ *rise the profile*.
- **damage** (động từ: làm hư hại; hoặc danh từ không đếm được) ↔ **damages** (danh từ số nhiều = **tiền bồi thường do toà tuyên**). *The court awarded her damages* — không bao giờ là *damage* ở nghĩa này.
- **efficient** (làm việc gọn, không tốn công vô ích — nói về người, hệ thống, quy trình) ↔ **effective** (đạt được kết quả mong muốn — nói về biện pháp, quảng cáo, thuốc). Một hệ thống lưu trữ *efficient*; một chiến dịch quảng cáo *effective*.
- Họ từ **econom-**: **economic** = thuộc về kinh tế (*economic development*) · **economical** = tiết kiệm, không tốn kém (*economical prices*) · **economics** = môn kinh tế học (số nhiều nhưng chia động từ số ít) · **economies** = các khoản tiết kiệm (*make economies* = cắt giảm chi phí).

</details>

### Bài 6 · Tính từ *-ed* và *-ing*

Điền câu bằng các từ trong hộp. Mỗi từ dùng một lần.

**Hộp từ:** confused · confusing · satisfied · satisfying · surprised · surprising · tired · tiring

1. She felt ______ after working hard all day.
2. We had an extremely ______ meal at Marco's restaurant.
3. Are you ______ with your progress on the course?
4. The London Underground can be very ______ the first time you use it.
5. Working 16 hours a day with children as a holiday rep can be very ______.
6. A ______ number of people actually complain about hotel hairdryers.
7. It is complicated, so if you're ______ about anything, don't hesitate to phone me.
8. I wasn't ______ to hear about their staff shortages because the pay is very low.

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 6</summary>

1 tired · 2 satisfying · 3 satisfied · 4 confusing · 5 tiring · 6 surprising · 7 confused · 8 surprised

**Điểm cần nhớ — một câu hỏi duy nhất là đủ**

Hỏi: **chỗ này đang nói về người *cảm thấy*, hay về thứ *gây ra* cảm giác đó?**

- **-ed → người cảm thấy.** *She felt **tired***. *Are you **satisfied***? *if you're **confused***. *I wasn't **surprised***.
- **-ing → thứ gây ra cảm giác.** *a **satisfying** meal* (bữa ăn làm ta hài lòng). *The Underground is **confusing*** (nó làm ta rối). *The job is **tiring*** (nó làm ta mệt). *a **surprising** number* (con số làm ta ngạc nhiên).

Câu 1 và 5 cùng gốc *tire* nhưng khác hẳn: *She felt **tired*** = cô ấy mệt · *The work is **tiring*** = công việc gây mệt. Nói *I am boring* nghĩa là "tôi là người nhạt nhẽo", chứ không phải "tôi đang thấy nhàm" (*I am bored*) — lỗi này đổi hẳn ý bạn muốn nói.

Giới từ đi kèm: *satisfied **with*** · *confused **about*** · *surprised **to** hear / **at** something*.

</details>

### Bài 7 · *have/get something done*

Hoàn thành bảng dịch vụ khách sạn bằng **have/get + tân ngữ + past participle**, dùng các cụm trong hộp.

**Hộp cụm:** bring a drink · clean them · cut your hair · deliver your meal · translate your documents · wash and iron your clothes

**Room service** — For everything you need, just contact reception using the phone at the side of your bed. For example, if you're hungry, you can (1) ______ to your room.

**Shoe cleaning** — Leave your shoes in the corridor at night and (2) ______ by morning.

**Laundry** — You can (3) ______; just complete the form in the wardrobe and leave it on the bed.

**Beauty salon** — (4) ______ and your nails manicured at the same time. Just phone reception to make an appointment.

**Night porter** — (5) ______ to your room after the bar has closed.

**Business facilities** — You can (6) ______ into a foreign language. Please give 48 hours' notice.

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 7</summary>

1. you can **have your meal delivered** to your room
2. and **have them cleaned** by morning
3. You can **have your clothes washed and ironed**
4. **Have your hair cut** and your nails manicured at the same time
5. **Have a drink brought** to your room after the bar has closed
6. You can **have your documents translated** into a foreign language

**Điểm cần nhớ**

- Công thức: **have / get + tân ngữ + past participle** = thu xếp cho **người khác** làm việc đó cho mình. *I had my hair cut* = tôi đi cắt tóc (thợ cắt), khác hẳn *I cut my hair* = tôi tự cắt.
- **Trật tự từ là chỗ dễ sai nhất:** tân ngữ đứng **giữa**. ✅ *have your meal delivered* ❌ *have delivered your meal*.
- *get* thân mật hơn *have*, nghĩa như nhau: *get these carpets cleaned!*
- Muốn nêu rõ ai làm thì dùng **get someone to do** hoặc **have someone do**: *We'll get an electrician to fix those lights.* · *They had the bedrooms refurbished **by** an exclusive firm.*
- Đừng lẫn với **make someone do something** (bắt buộc — và sau *make* không có *to*).
- Chú ý câu 4: *Have your hair cut* — *cut* là past participle của *cut*, trông giống nguyên thể nên nhiều người tưởng sai.

</details>

### Bài 8 · Multi-word verbs

Điền câu bằng các multi-word verb trong hộp, chia đúng thì.

**Hộp từ:** catch up · get through · pass on · run out · see to · sort out

1. I've been trying to ______ to him all morning but the line's engaged.
2. She's staying late at the office to ______ on some reports.
3. Your passport will ______ next month. You must get it renewed.
4. 'These payments need to be checked.' 'I'll ______ them this afternoon.'
5. We've managed to ______ the computer system's initial problems.
6. I'll ______ your message to Mr Barber as soon as he gets back from lunch.

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 8</summary>

1 get through · 2 catch up · 3 run out · 4 see to · 5 sort out · 6 pass on

**Điểm cần nhớ — đây là bộ từ dùng qua điện thoại, học kèm giới từ**

- **get through to somebody** = gọi được, liên lạc được. *I can't get through to him.* Cùng chủ đề: *the line's engaged* (máy đang bận, Anh) = *the line's busy* (Mỹ).
- **catch up on something** = làm cho kịp phần đang tồn đọng. *catch up on some reports*. Khác **catch up with somebody** = gặp lại ai để cập nhật tình hình.
- **run out** = hết, cạn. Không có tân ngữ: *Your passport will run out.* Có tân ngữ thì thêm *of*: *We've run out of brochures.*
- **see to something** = lo, xử lý việc đó. *I'll see to them this afternoon.*
- **sort out a problem** = giải quyết xong vấn đề.
- **pass on a message to somebody** = chuyển lời. Tân ngữ là danh từ thì đặt đâu cũng được (*pass on your message* / *pass your message on*), nhưng nếu là **đại từ** thì **buộc** phải tách: *I'll pass **it** on.* ❌ *pass on it*.

Bộ còn lại của Unit 9 nên nhớ cùng lúc: *put somebody through* (nối máy) · *hold on* (giữ máy) · *get back to somebody* (liên lạc lại) · *be cut off* (bị ngắt) · *hang up* (cúp máy) · *look into* (xem xét).

</details>

### Bài 9 · Linking words

Điền các từ nối trong hộp. Mỗi từ dùng một lần. Chú ý chữ hoa khi từ nối đứng đầu câu.

**Hộp từ:** although · as a result · furthermore · however · on the whole

1. The hotel tax for hotels in Venice is based on their star rating. ______, there may be extra charges depending on the dates of your stay and the location of the hotel in the city.
2. The museum was free but now there is an entrance fee. ______, the number of visitors has gone down.
3. ______, the festival was a great success despite the bad weather.
4. Until 1944, women in France were denied the right to vote. ______, they could not open a bank account or cash a cheque without their husband's permission.
5. I can't help admiring Picasso's technique, ______ I don't like his paintings very much.

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 9</summary>

1 **However** · 2 **As a result** · 3 **On the whole** · 4 **Furthermore** · 5 **although**

**Điểm cần nhớ — bốn nhóm từ nối, phân theo chức năng**

| Chức năng | Từ nối | Chấm câu |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Thêm ý cùng chiều | furthermore · in addition · moreover · what's more | đứng đầu câu mới, theo sau là dấu phẩy |
| Nêu kết quả | as a result · consequently · therefore | đứng đầu câu mới, theo sau là dấu phẩy |
| Nêu tương phản | however · in contrast · nevertheless | đứng đầu câu mới, theo sau là dấu phẩy |
| Nêu tương phản trong **một** câu | although · though · whereas · while | nối hai mệnh đề, **không** có dấu chấm trước |
| Tổng kết, nhìn chung | on the whole · overall · by and large | đứng đầu câu |

**Lỗi hay gặp nhất:** dùng *however* như *but*. ❌ *I like his technique, however I don't like his paintings.* Phải là ✅ *…, **although** I don't like…* hoặc ✅ *I like his technique. **However**, I don't like his paintings.*

**although** là liên từ nối hai mệnh đề; **despite** / **in spite of** là giới từ, theo sau là **danh từ hoặc V-ing**: *despite the bad weather* ✅ · *despite it was raining* ❌ (phải là *although it was raining*).

</details>

### Bài 10 · Asking questions — sửa lỗi

Sửa lỗi trong các câu hỏi dưới đây.

1. Can I ask how old are you?
2. It's too early, is not it?
3. Does he wants the executive suite?
4. Do you mind if I'm asking you to move?
5. Would you mind to share a room?
6. What exactly does seems to be the problem?
7. Could you tell me where do you live?
8. I am wondering if you would agree to replace Fyodor?

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 10</summary>

1. Can I ask how old **you are**?
2. It's too early, **isn't it**?
3. Does he **want** the executive suite?
4. Do you mind if **I ask** you to move?
5. Would you mind **sharing** a room?
6. What exactly **seems** to be the problem?
7. Could you tell me where **you live**?
8. **I was wondering** if you would agree to replace Fyodor**.**

**Điểm cần nhớ — năm luật, hết**

1. **Câu hỏi gián tiếp giữ trật tự câu kể.** Sau *Can I ask…*, *Could you tell me…*, *Do you know…* thì **không đảo** chủ ngữ và không thêm *do/does*. ✅ *Could you tell me where you live?* ❌ *…where do you live?* (câu 1 và 7)
2. **Câu hỏi đuôi luôn viết tắt.** ✅ *isn't it?* ❌ *is not it?* (câu 2)
3. **Sau trợ động từ *do/does/did* dùng động từ nguyên thể.** ✅ *Does he want…* ❌ *Does he wants…* (câu 3)
4. **Khi từ hỏi *who / what / which* chính là chủ ngữ thì không dùng *do/does*.** ✅ *What seems to be the problem?* ❌ *What does seems…* So sánh: *What **do** you want?* (*what* là tân ngữ → cần *do*) với *What **happened**?* (*what* là chủ ngữ → không cần). (câu 6)
5. **Sau *mind* dùng V-ing.** ✅ *Would you mind sharing a room?* ❌ *…to share*. Còn *Do you mind if…* thì theo sau là **hiện tại đơn**: ✅ *Do you mind if I ask…* ❌ *…if I'm asking*. (câu 4 và 5)

Câu 8 là lỗi khác: *I was wondering if…* là **câu đề nghị lịch sự**, không phải câu hỏi — kết bằng **dấu chấm**, không phải dấu hỏi. Dùng thì quá khứ *was* làm câu nhẹ hơn hẳn *I am wondering*. Đây là mẫu câu đáng học thuộc cho lễ tân: *I was wondering if you would mind changing your room tomorrow night.*

</details>

### Bài 11 · Personal qualities — tiền tố phủ định

Các câu sau vô nghĩa. Viết lại phẩm chất ở dạng **phủ định** bằng tiền tố thích hợp.

1. Many hotel guests are *honest* and steal the towels.
2. They're terribly *efficient* because it took five phone calls and a letter of complaint before they finally decided to react.
3. It would be *appropriate* of me to comment on the situation until I know more of the facts.
4. She was awfully *polite* and just took the tickets off me without saying thank you.
5. He's totally *competent*. He never files anything and often loses documents.
6. I dislike staying in branded hotels; they're so *personal* and *welcoming*.
7. No one wants to work with a person who is *reliable* and *capable* of correcting his or her mistakes.
8. The passengers were getting more and more *patient* as the flight was soon to take off and many of them had not checked in.

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 11</summary>

1 **dishonest** · 2 **inefficient** · 3 **inappropriate** · 4 **impolite** · 5 **incompetent** · 6 **impersonal** và **unwelcoming** · 7 **unreliable** và **incapable** · 8 **impatient**

**Điểm cần nhớ — tiền tố nào đi với từ nào**

| Tiền tố | Dùng khi | Ví dụ |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **im-** | trước **m** và **p** | impolite · impatient · impersonal · impossible |
| **in-** | mặc định cho từ gốc Latin | inappropriate · inefficient · incompetent · incapable |
| **il-** | trước **l** | illegal · illegible · illogical |
| **ir-** | trước **r** | irresponsible · irregular · irrelevant |
| **un-** | mặc định cho từ gốc Anh | unwelcoming · unfriendly · unreliable · unhelpful |
| **dis-** | nghĩa "ngược lại, mất đi" | dishonest · disloyal · disrespectful |

Không có quy tắc tuyệt đối, nhưng **im- trước m/p, il- trước l, ir- trước r** thì gần như luôn đúng — ba luật này xử lý phần lớn trường hợp. Còn lại chọn giữa *in-* và *un-*: từ nhìn "Latin" (*-ent*, *-ate*, *-ible*, *-able*) thường lấy *in-*; từ nhìn "Anh" hoặc là phân từ (*-ing*, *-ed*, *-ful*, *-y*) thường lấy *un-*.

Bộ từ tích cực đầy đủ của Unit 9, đáng học kèm: welcoming · friendly · polite · patient · reliable · competent · efficient · honest · loyal · respectful · appropriate · well-groomed (ăn mặc chỉn chu).

</details>

### Bài 12 · Past simple hay past perfect

Chia động từ trong ngoặc ở **past simple** hoặc **past perfect**.

Two years ago, I (1) ______ *(be)* on my way to spend the holidays with my fiancé, who (2) ______ *(live)* in Boston at the time. I (3) ______ *(feel)* really tired because I (4) ______ *(be)* to a party the night before.

At the airport, I (5) ______ *(run into)* a colleague who (6) ______ *(be)* on her way to Buffalo. She (7) ______ *(tell)* me that she (8) ______ *(arrive)* there the previous day and that no flights (9) ______ *(take off)* since Thursday because of the snowstorms. It (10) ______ *(look like)* we would have to wait a long time. We couldn't do any shopping because most of the stores (11) ______ *(close)* early owing to a lack of personnel.

My co-worker (12) ______ *(have)* a classical guitar with her, and I (13) ______ *(take)* singing lessons before I (14) ______ *(abandon)* the idea of becoming an American Maria Callas. The people around us (15) ______ *(look)* really fed up, so we (16) ______ *(decide)* to give an improvised concert to cheer up all the passengers who (17) ______ *(miss)* their flights.

This (18) ______ *(last)* for about three-quarters of an hour, and then we (19) ______ *(hear)* that the ground staff (20) ______ *(clear)* the runway and everybody could finally check in.

Then, to my horror, I suddenly (21) ______ *(remember)* — I (22) ______ *(leave)* my passport on the kitchen table. I (23) ______ *(put)* it there so I wouldn't forget it! So I (24) ______ *(go)* back home, (25) ______ *(make)* myself a lonely dinner and (26) ______ *(talk)* for hours with my fiancé on the phone. It was by far the worst holiday I (27) ______ *(ever spend)*.

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 12</summary>

1 was · 2 was living *(hoặc lived)* · 3 felt · 4 had been · 5 ran into · 6 was · 7 told · 8 had arrived · 9 had taken off · 10 looked like · 11 had closed · 12 had · 13 had taken · 14 abandoned · 15 looked · 16 decided · 17 had missed · 18 lasted · 19 heard · 20 had cleared · 21 remembered · 22 had left · 23 had put · 24 went · 25 made · 26 talked · 27 had ever spent

**Điểm cần nhớ — cách quyết định trong một giây**

Câu chuyện chạy trên **past simple**. Chuyển sang **past perfect** chỉ khi bạn **lùi lại trước** mốc đang kể:

- (4) *I felt tired because I **had been** to a party the night before* — đi tiệc xảy ra trước lúc cảm thấy mệt.
- (8) (9) *She told me she **had arrived** the previous day and no flights **had taken off** since Thursday* — hai việc này xảy ra trước lúc cô ấy kể.
- (11) (17) (20) *the stores **had closed*** · *passengers who **had missed** their flights* · *the ground staff **had cleared** the runway* — đều xong trước mốc đang kể.
- (22) (23) *I **had left** my passport… I **had put** it there* — cao trào của chuyện: hai việc này xảy ra trước lúc cô ấy chợt nhớ ra.
- (27) *the worst holiday I **had ever spent*** — sau so sánh nhất, mốc quy chiếu là quá khứ nên dùng past perfect (nếu mốc là hiện tại thì *have ever spent*).

**Dấu hiệu nhận biết:** *the night before*, *the previous day*, *since*, *before*, *already*, *just*, *by the time* — thấy những từ này là gần như chắc chắn cần past perfect.

- (13) (14) *I **had taken** singing lessons **before** I **abandoned** the idea* — có *before*, nhưng chỉ **một** trong hai vế ở past perfect: vế xảy ra trước. Khi *before* hoặc *after* đã nói rõ thứ tự thì dùng past simple cả hai cũng được chấp nhận; past perfect chỉ làm thứ tự rõ hơn.
- (2) *who **was living** in Boston at the time* — *at the time* chỉ một tình trạng đang diễn ra → past continuous là tự nhiên nhất, nhưng *lived* cũng đúng.
- (5) *ran into* = tình cờ gặp. Đừng dịch máy móc thành "chạy vào".

</details>

### Bài 13 · Sửa mười lỗi chính tả

Đọc đoạn trích brochure về Princess Miranda Conference Centre ở Rio de Janeiro. Tìm và sửa **mười** lỗi chính tả.

Located in downtown Rio and closed to the business distract, this brand-new accomodation comprises a total of 74 suits and 114 bedrooms. The conference centre is within waking distance of the Tiradentes Palace and the National Library. Nearby points of intrest include teh famous Carioca Aqueduct and the Santa Teresa quarter with its cafés and restaurants.

Each room has a fully stoked fridge and a direct-dial telephone with voicemale. Bathroom features include baths and/or showers, bidets and hairdryers.

The international airport is just 25 minutes away by car or shuttel bus.

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 13</summary>

| Sai | Đúng | Vì sao |
| --- | --- | --- |
| closed to | **close to** | *close to* = gần. *closed* là "đã đóng" — nghĩa ngược hẳn |
| distract | **district** | *distract* = làm phân tâm |
| accomodation | **accommodation** | hai chữ **m**. Đây là từ viết sai nhiều nhất trong ngành |
| suits | **suites** | *suite* /swiːt/ = phòng suite; *suit* /suːt/ = bộ đồ |
| waking | **walking** | *within walking distance* = đi bộ tới được — cụm cố định |
| intrest | **interest** | *points of interest* = điểm đáng tham quan |
| teh | **the** | lỗi đánh máy đảo chữ |
| stoked | **stocked** | *a fully stocked fridge* = tủ lạnh đầy đồ |
| voicemale | **voicemail** | *mail*, không phải *male* |
| shuttel | **shuttle** | *shuttle bus* = xe đưa đón |

**Điểm cần nhớ — mấy từ ngành du lịch hay viết sai nhất**

**accommodation** (hai m, hai c) · **restaurant** · **itinerary** · **brochure** · **occupancy** (hai c) · **recommend** (hai m, một c) · **necessary** (một c, hai s) · **separate** (*sep-a-rate*, không phải *seperate*) · **liaise** · **questionnaire** (hai n).

Cả mười lỗi ở trên đều là loại mà công cụ kiểm tra chính tả **không bắt được**, vì *closed*, *suits*, *waking*, *stoked*, *male* đều là từ có thật. Vì thế Writing Bank nhắc: *Always proofread your letter* — đọc lại bằng mắt, đừng tin phần mềm.

</details>

### Bài 14 · Idioms và metaphors

Hoàn thành các idiom bằng từ trong hộp.

**Hộp từ:** bill · bone · horizon · roof · shoestring

1. I think there are probably more price rises on the ______.
2. The book tells you how to plan a visit on a ______ budget.
3. Who is going to foot the ______ for all the extra security measures?
4. We can't make any more economies — everything's already been cut to the ______.
5. Because of the speculation, the price of raw materials has gone through the ______.

<details class="key"><summary>✅ Đáp án — Bài 14</summary>

1 horizon · 2 shoestring · 3 bill · 4 bone · 5 roof

**Điểm cần nhớ — nghĩa và cách dùng**

| Idiom | Nghĩa | Dùng khi |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **on the horizon** | đang ló ở phía trước, sắp tới | dự báo xu hướng: *more price rises on the horizon* · *hazards on the horizon* |
| **on a shoestring** | với ngân sách rất ít | *travel on a shoestring* · *a shoestring budget* |
| **foot the bill** | đứng ra chi trả | ai gánh chi phí: *Who is going to foot the bill?* |
| **cut to the bone** | cắt tới mức không thể cắt nữa | *costs have been cut to the bone* |
| **go through the roof** | tăng vọt | giá, nhu cầu, chi phí tăng đột biến |

Bốn idiom còn lại của Unit 10 nên nhớ cùng bộ: **put the brakes on something** (hãm lại) · **greet somebody with open arms** (đón nồng nhiệt) · **get back on the road** (trở lại với các chuyến đi) · **in the front row** (ở hàng đầu, tức nhóm ưu tiên nhất).

**Cảnh báo dùng idiom:** những cụm này rất hiệu quả trong **bài báo, bài nói trước đồng nghiệp, small talk**. Nhưng đừng đưa vào **report, proposal hay email cho khách** — ở đó văn phải trung tính. Viết *costs have been reduced significantly*, không viết *cut to the bone*.

</details>

## Writing Bank

> ✍️ **Chín loại văn bản bạn sẽ phải viết trong nghề** (sách giấy tr. 96–101). Mỗi mục gồm: **Cách viết** (luật của loại văn bản đó) và **Bài mẫu** (văn bản thật để bắt chước). Case study của các unit đều dẫn về đây.

| Loại văn bản | Dùng ở | Trang sách |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Brochure descriptions](#brochure-descriptions) | Unit 1 · Case study — mô tả gói cưới Virgin Islands | 96 |
| [Press or media release](#press-or-media-release) | Unit 4 · Case study — quảng bá Eldorado | 96 |
| [Catalogue entries](#catalogue-entries) | Unit 5 — viết entry cho catalogue bảo tàng | 97 |
| [Audio commentary](#audio-commentary-cho-bảo-tàng-hoặc-phòng-tranh) | Unit 5 — soạn lời cho audio guide | 97 |
| [Minutes of a meeting](#minutes-of-a-meeting) | Unit 2 · Case study — biên bản họp CheapSky | 98 |
| [Emails](#emails) | Unit 9 · Case study — trả lời comment card | 99 |
| [Proposals](#proposals) | Unit 7 · Case study — proposal ứng cử danh hiệu văn hoá | 100 |
| [Reports](#reports) | Unit 8 · Case study — report về khách sạn Howatt | 101 |
| [Responding to a complaint](#responding-to-a-complaint) | Unit 3 · Case study — thư xin lỗi · Unit 9 | 101 |

> 💡 **Cách dùng mục này để tiến bộ nhanh nhất:** đọc *Cách viết*, che bài mẫu lại, tự viết bản của mình, **rồi mới** mở bài mẫu ra so. So ba thứ: bố cục các đoạn, mẫu câu mở và kết, và những cụm bạn chưa nghĩ ra được. Chép lại các cụm đó vào sổ riêng.

### Brochure descriptions

#### Cách viết

Brochure và catalogue entry tồn tại để làm một nơi nghe thật tuyệt và khiến người đọc mơ về một kỳ nghỉ hoàn hảo. Vì vậy văn brochure **đầy tính từ mạnh và cấu trúc so sánh nhất**.

- Mỗi danh từ đi kèm ít nhất một tính từ: *golden beaches*, *glorious coastline*, *opulent rooms*, *tranquil lake*.
- Dùng so sánh nhất và cụm tuyệt đối: *one of the world's most exclusive destinations*, *the finest choice*, *second to none*.
- Động từ cũng phải "sang": *stretch*, *nestle*, *overlook*, *boast*, *feature*, *offer*.
- Nói với người đọc bằng *you* và câu mệnh lệnh: *Enjoy breathtaking views…*
- Tránh từ nhạt. Đổi: *nice* → *white sandy* · *decent* → *luxury* · *lovely* → *perfect* · *clear* → *sparkling* · *green* → *lush* · *good views* → *spectacular* · *different* → *exotic*.

#### Bài mẫu

The golden beaches stretch for 60 beautiful miles of this fascinating country's glorious coastline. One of the world's most exclusive destinations, this is a perfect place to spend a luxurious and romantic honeymoon.

Nestled against the dramatic background of the imposing Aravalli mountain range, the Ramachandran is a majestic hotel set in a stunning location. The opulent rooms overlook the tranquil Lake Pichola, which is an ideal spot for a picnic or a leisurely boat ride on the peaceful, turquoise-blue waters.

The Shahjehan is situated in serene, landscaped gardens featuring cascading fountains and elegant courtyards. Enjoy breathtaking views of the Taj Mahal as you sit out on the terrace after a day spent among the bustling streets of Agra. The superb restaurant offers the finest choice of both Indian and international cuisine.

### Press or media release

#### Cách viết

Press release dùng để công bố sản phẩm mới, sự kiện đang lên kế hoạch, thành tích vừa đạt được, hoặc thông tin về một điểm đến. Bảy tiêu chí:

- **Ngắn và đi thẳng vào việc** — không quá 400 từ.
- Viết thành câu hoàn chỉnh, dấu câu đúng — không viết kiểu gạch đầu dòng rời rạc.
- Phải có **một tiêu đề hút mắt**.
- **Đoạn đầu chứa thông tin đáng đưa tin nhất** — nhà báo chỉ đọc đoạn đầu.
- Kết bằng **thông tin liên hệ** của tổ chức phát hành: địa chỉ thư, email và số điện thoại.
- Nên **lưu lại toàn bộ press release trong một khu vực lưu trữ trên website** của mình.
- Kèm câu trích dẫn của một người có chức danh — nhà báo rất hay dùng lại nguyên câu đó.

#### Bài mẫu

**Announcing Machuci's brand-new digital magazine**

This first issue of the Machuci.com digital magazine features the contestants of the Tribal Warfare adventure reality TV series, with exclusive interviews about their jungle challenges. In addition, the magazine highlights the country's many up-market hotels, resorts and activities, including the newly-opened Altos de Santa Teresa Luxury Spa & Beauty Treatment Resort.

The digital magazine is in both English and Spanish and is the latest resource for anyone looking for information on Machuci. Its cutting-edge CSS3 format enables display on all smartphone, mobile and tablet devices. In addition, the magazine is integrated with the LinkedIn, Twitter and Facebook pages of Machuci's National Tourism Organization (NTO).

According to José Rodriguez, Public Relations Manager of Machuci's NTO, 'the digital magazine is the next step in providing information for those interested in making Machuci one of the world's most popular destinations'.

Machuci's tourism infrastructure boasts luxury properties as well as intimate boutique hotels and family-run guesthouses in all the major centres, as well as stunning mountain and eco-resorts. The country now enjoys one of the lowest tourist crime records on the continent, so visitors can rest assured their holiday will be trouble-free from start to finish.

For further information on submissions and advertising opportunities, please contact José.Rodriguez@Machuci.com · Tel: 011 505 555 4841

### Catalogue entries

#### Cách viết

Catalogue in ra để đi kèm triển lãm, thường trực hay tạm thời. Nội dung thay đổi tuỳ mục đích: có entry nói về giai đoạn nghệ thuật và cuộc đời hoạ sĩ, có entry mô tả bức tranh và những gì nó thể hiện.

Bố cục ba phần dùng được cho hầu hết mọi trường hợp:

1. **Dòng định danh** — tên tác phẩm, năm, tác giả, chất liệu và kích thước: *Oil on canvas, 100 cm × 65 cm*.
2. **Tác giả** — năm sinh, năm mất, quốc tịch, nơi làm việc, trường phái, những người cùng thời.
3. **Tác phẩm** — chất liệu, màu sắc, cách xử lý, ý nghĩa; và với tác phẩm có lịch sử sưu tầm thì thêm ai đặt hàng, ai từng sở hữu.

Văn phong: **bị động** để trọng tâm nằm ở tác phẩm (*was commissioned by*, *were displayed in*), và **thì quá khứ** cho cuộc đời tác giả, **hiện tại** cho cái người xem đang thấy trước mắt (*The landscape is depicted…*).

#### Bài mẫu 1

**Landscape and Houses at Céret (1913) by Juan Gris** — Oil on canvas, 100 cm × 65 cm

Juan Gris (1887–1927) was a Spanish painter and sculptor who lived and worked in Paris for most of his life. He was a friend of some of the most famous painters of the early 20th century, including Matisse, Braque, Fernand Léger, Modigliani and his fellow countryman Pablo Picasso. He is regarded as one of the founder members of the artistic movement known as Synthetic Cubism.

Gris favoured bold, vibrant colours, as in the painting here of Céret, a village in the Pyrenees in south-west France. The landscape is depicted not as the eye normally 'sees' it but as the artist 'thinks' it, deprived of volume and portrayed in a series of two-dimensional planes. The canvas is designed as an abstract geometrical structure, into which Gris has inserted different features of the countryside — houses, trees and fields.

#### Bài mẫu 2

**Return of the Bucintoro to the Molo on Ascension Day (1732) by Canaletto** — Oil on canvas, 77 cm × 126 cm

Canaletto (1697–1768) was the most famous Italian landscape painter of the 18th century. His pictures were great favourites with the British aristocracy, who purchased them as souvenirs during their Grand Tours of Italy, which were so popular at the time.

*The Return of the Bucintoro to the Molo on Ascension Day* was one of a series of paintings commissioned by Joseph Smith, a British businessman living in Venice. Canaletto's works were displayed in Smith's house, where he introduced them to potential buyers. Kings and queens such as Catherine the Great of Russia and George III of England possessed substantial collections.

### Audio commentary cho bảo tàng hoặc phòng tranh

#### Cách viết

Audio commentary được viết **để đọc lên**, nên phải dùng ngôn ngữ tương đối đơn giản và dễ hiểu. Câu không được dài, để người thu âm đọc trôi và người nghe theo được.

Bốn điều phân biệt audio commentary với catalogue entry:

- **Định hướng người nghe trước tiên.** Họ đang đứng trong phòng và không biết nhìn đâu: *Look now at the seated scribe in front of you.*
- **Câu ngắn, một ý một câu.** Người đọc không thể lùi lại nghe lại dễ dàng như đọc chữ.
- **Dùng từ thông dụng, không dùng từ Latin trang trọng.** Viết *built*, không viết *constructed*. Xem [Review 2 · Bài 3](#bài-3-từ-đồng-nghĩa-dễ-hơn).
- **Kết bằng một chỉ dẫn sang hiện vật tiếp theo:** *Now turn round and look at the display containing…*

#### Bài mẫu

Look now at the seated scribe in front of you. In the complex society of Ancient Egypt, the scribe was a very important person, because he was one of the few people who was able to read and write.

A scribe studied for about 12 years to learn and master the 700 hieroglyphs that were commonly used. First of all, they spent a long time copying individual signs and passages from textbooks. Later, they learnt to write letters in different styles — accountancy, mathematics and astrology — all subjects they would need in their future careers.

For most people, the word *scribe* refers to someone in the act of writing. But it is also used to translate the word *sech*, which is the title of a high-ranking Egyptian official. These officials were responsible for the management of property and personnel. Government ministries and royal households employed hundreds of scribes who were responsible for the organization of agriculture and building activity. In the temples and pyramids, scribes were in charge of making a list of the objects contained inside them.

The Abusir archives, some of which are kept at this museum, provide valuable evidence of this intense activity. Without scribes, we would know very little of Egyptian culture and religion.

Now turn round and look at the display containing…

### Minutes of a meeting

#### Cách viết

Với phần lớn cuộc họp công việc, **action minutes** hữu ích hơn biên bản ghi lại toàn bộ lời nói. Nó ghi ngắn nội dung thảo luận, rồi nêu rõ **đã quyết gì** và **ai phải làm**.

- Lấy **agenda của cuộc họp làm sườn** cho biên bản.
- Đầu biên bản ghi **ngày, giờ, mục đích họp và danh sách người tham dự** (xem mẫu dưới).
- Trong thân biên bản, gọi người bằng **chữ viết tắt tên** thay vì tên đầy đủ.
- Tóm tắt ý chính của thảo luận **theo từng mục đã số hoá trong agenda**.
- Với mỗi mục, ghi thêm **quyết định gì đã được đưa ra và ai chịu trách nhiệm thực hiện**.
- Cuối biên bản ghi **ngày, giờ và địa điểm cuộc họp tiếp theo** nếu đã biết.
- **Đánh máy biên bản càng sớm càng tốt** sau khi họp, để không quên chi tiết.

Về ngôn ngữ: dùng **quá khứ với động từ tường thuật** (*told the meeting*, *reported*, *pointed out*, *proposed*, *requested*, *stated*, *agreed*) và **câu bị động hoặc *to*-infinitive cho việc phải làm** (*Mary Dodd **to contact** potential sponsors*, *This item **will be put** on the agenda*). Xem thêm Unit 8 · Key expressions về *have/get something done* trong biên bản.

#### Bài mẫu

**Tourism Advisory Board**

**Purpose of meeting:** To discuss local tourism initiatives
**Date/Time:** 14 January
**Present:** Jolanda Bossers, Vera Brown, Mary Dodd, Tim Mason, Demond Tibbs *(apologies: Bobby Brown)*
**Chairperson:** Mary Dodd

| # | Topic | Discussion | Action | Responsible / deadline |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Seafood festival | Mary Dodd told the meeting that, further to discussions with the Chamber of Commerce, the 1st seafood festival will be held in September to promote local seafood restaurants and offer samples of their specialities. | Tim Mason proposed a motion to donate £5,000 towards this event. Agreed unanimously. Mary Dodd to contact potential sponsors. | MD, 21 February |
| 2 | Historic village signage | Jolanda Bossers reported that she had met with County Manager Don Ward and Planning Director Ken Wilson to suggest new signage. They would like to create criteria for cultural and historic sites. Signs should promote the cultural and/or historical significance of the site, not the businesses located there. | Jolanda Bossers agreed to meet again with DW and KW to take this forward, and to suggest we incorporate GPS software, website information and interactive technology together with the sign. | JB, 30 June |
| 3 | Travel marketing report | Vera Brown pointed out that most of the key strategies put forward in the Executive Summary had been implemented, but more research needed to be done, especially on visitor expenditure. | Demond Tibbs requested that we also review the Regional Tourism Action Plan, developed in December. He would present the main points at the next meeting. | DT, 3 March |
| 4 | River Locke nature trail — progress report | There is a real need to complete the trail before the summer season. At present, access to the river is limited and there is very little infrastructure for riding or walking, especially for families. This trail will provide access for cycling, walking, fishing and water sports. | Mary Dodd stated that the aim is to have more funding in place before the next budget review. This item will be put on the agenda for the next meeting. | None |

The next meeting will be held on 3 March in the TAB offices, room 101.

### Emails

#### Cách viết

Văn phong email phụ thuộc vào **hoàn cảnh** và **quan hệ giữa người viết và người đọc**. Viết cho người không quen thì dùng giọng **neutral**; viết cho đồng nghiệp thân thì **informal**.

Cả hai giọng đều phải:

- **Điền dòng subject / reference.**
- Mở đầu bằng lời chào — *Dear Mr Baker*, *Dear Ms Sanchez*, *Hi Shara*.
- **Nói rõ mục đích của email** ngay đoạn đầu.
- Giữ email **tương đối ngắn**.
- **Lịch sự** — *Could you please…?* · *I would be grateful if…* · *I was wondering if…*
- **Soát lỗi chính tả và lỗi từ vựng** rõ ràng.
- **Kiểm tra lại địa chỉ email người nhận.**
- **Nhắc tới tệp đính kèm trong thân email** — và nhớ đính kèm thật.
- **Đoạn cuối nói rõ việc gì diễn ra tiếp theo** — ví dụ *Please let me know by the beginning of next week.*
- **Kết đúng cách.** Các cách kết thông dụng: *Yours sincerely* · *Best regards* · *Best wishes* · *Regards*.
- Nếu **không biết tên người nhận**, dùng quy ước của thư trang trọng: mở *Dear Sir / Dear Madam* và kết *Yours faithfully*.

**Khác biệt giữa hai giọng** — nhìn hai bài mẫu dưới rồi so:

| | Neutral | Informal |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Lời chào | *Dear Mrs Metzler* | *Hi Steve* |
| Viết tắt | không dùng | dùng: *pls*, *re*, *asap*, *pax* |
| Câu | đầy đủ, dài hơn | ngắn, đi thẳng |
| Kết | *Yours sincerely* + họ tên và chức danh | *Regards* + tên gọi thân |

#### Bài mẫu 1 — neutral style

**From:** victoria.green@adventureafrica.com
**To:** metzler.valerie99@yahoo.com
**Subject:** Safari tours in Kenya
**Date:** 11 October

Dear Mrs Metzler

Thank you for your enquiry concerning our range of safari tours in Kenya. They all start from Nairobi — you make your own way there — and take in Rwanda, Burundi and the Democratic Republic of the Congo. Depending on the length of time you would like to spend on safari, we will send you a concept contract and all the specific requirements for travelling in Africa, such as visas, vaccinations and recommended clothing.

Many of our clients choose to combine a safari with a stay in Mombasa on the Indian Ocean. If you so wish, we can arrange for reasonably-priced accommodation there, and you could travel back on the famous overnight sleeper train called the Jambo Kenya Deluxe, which takes you through the Tsavo game park.

If you would like to contact me with your dates, number of companions, etc., I will send you the full details.

I look forward to hearing from you.

Yours sincerely

Victoria Green
Travel Consultant

#### Bài mẫu 2 — informal style

**From:** fiona.harding@travelexpert.co.uk
**To:** stephen.wainright@sicilysun.co.it
**Subject:** Baggage handling
**Date:** 8/11/13

Hi Steve

Following on from our exchange of emails yesterday, could you pls advise me on your position re transporting baggage? Surely we cannot expect 172 pax to collect all their bags on arrival and walk 500 metres to the bus pick-up point.

Could you please get back to me asap?

Regards

Fi

> 📌 **Từ viết tắt trong email nội bộ ngành:** *pls* = please · *re* = regarding (về việc) · *asap* = as soon as possible · *pax* = passengers · *ETA* = estimated time of arrival · *FOC* = free of charge · *TBC* = to be confirmed. Dùng với đồng nghiệp thì được; **tuyệt đối không dùng khi viết cho khách.**

### Proposals

#### Cách viết

Proposal phải viết bằng ngôn ngữ **trang trọng và trung tính**, và càng súc tích càng tốt. Nên mở bằng một **executive summary** nêu ngắn gọn **đề xuất làm gì và vì sao**. Sau đó là phần chi tiết hơn về **cách thực hiện**, rồi đến **lợi ích và ưu điểm** của việc đề xuất.

Bốn phần, theo đúng thứ tự này:

1. **Executive summary** — đề xuất gì, vì sao bây giờ, vì sao ở đây. Người quyết định có thể chỉ đọc phần này.
2. **Objective** — mục tiêu cụ thể, đo được.
3. **Methodology** — sẽ làm những gì, theo bước nào. Dùng **bị động ở tương lai**: *A database will be generated…* · *Tour routes will be designed…* · *On-site visits will be carried out…*
4. **Benefits** — lợi ích kinh tế, văn hoá, xã hội. Nói bằng số nếu có.

#### Bài mẫu

**Gastronomy Tours in the Lomagne Region**

**Executive Summary**

This proposal aims to make a case for the development of art and gastronomy tours as a sustainable tourism strategy for our region. In recent years, the search for new and innovative products has led to the rediscovery of cultural tourism in all its aspects. However, one aspect that reflects the ancestral customs, history, geography and economic and social development of our region has not yet been fully explored — that of gastronomy.

**Objective**

This proposal aims to develop this new tourist product by researching tourism routes that integrate diverse cultural attractions, with a special emphasis on local gastronomy.

**Methodology**

The methodology will be as follows:

- A database will be generated of all the cultural and gastronomic attractions in the region, together with the accommodation infrastructure.
- Tour routes will be designed combining art, rural traditions and gastronomy, as well as other aspects of interest to potential visitors.
- On-site visits will be carried out to check that the services and attractions offer a minimum standard of quality, and that the times estimated for the visits in the context of a package tour are feasible.

**Economic benefits**

The programme has the potential to increase the number of travellers interested in cultural tourism, both of national and international origin, and therefore to increase the revenue from tourism in the region. In many of the areas we propose to research the availability of suitable accommodation on the one hand, and the ethnic, cultural and gastronomic wealth on the other, and to offer an adequate framework within which to implement projects of low-impact sustainable tourism.

This proposal aims to preserve both the cultural and natural resources in the region, while at the same time bringing substantial economic benefits in terms of employment opportunities, transport infrastructure and foreign currency revenue.

### Reports

#### Cách viết

Report phải được **tổ chức rõ ràng, theo trình tự logic**, và ngôn ngữ dùng phải **trang trọng và trung tính**. Bộ tiêu đề dưới đây phù hợp với phần lớn report ngắn — dùng đúng bốn tiêu đề này, đừng tự nghĩ ra tiêu đề khác:

| Tiêu đề | Trả lời câu hỏi | Thì thường dùng |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Background** | Ai yêu cầu report này, để làm gì, dựa trên dữ liệu nào? | present perfect bị động — *This report has been written at the request of…* |
| **Findings** | Tìm ra được gì? | present simple — *The findings indicate that…* |
| **Conclusion** | Nghĩa của những phát hiện đó là gì? | present simple, kèm *Although…* để cân hai mặt |
| **Recommendations** | Nên làm gì tiếp? | *must*, *should*, *We recommend that…* |

Ba luật về ngôn ngữ: **không viết tắt** (*do not*, không phải *don't*); **không dùng *I*** — dùng *This report*, *Our market research*, *We recommend*; **luôn kèm số** nếu có (*up 0.8 percent to 133,201*, *just 1.6 days*). Số làm report đáng tin, tính từ thì không.

#### Bài mẫu

**Report: The Tourism Potential of Santa Rosa** — 18 March 2013

**Background**

This report has been written at the request of the Regional Tourism Development Group to investigate the tourism potential of a seaside town on the southwest coast. A quantitative and qualitative approach has been used, drawing on data on domestic tourist trips within the region, including places visited, activities, attractions, accommodation and transport.

**Findings**

The findings indicate that tourists are now looking for more than just a short break; they are seeking a complete experience, with opportunities for sightseeing, a good standard of service and a pleasant environment. Our market research has shown, however, that the town simply does not have enough attractions and places of interest. The average visitor stays here for just 1.6 days, and a shopping trip to the market and a visit to the Ocean Experience are still the main attractions.

**Conclusion**

Although domestic guest nights are up 0.8 percent to 133,201 and the average length of stay has increased from 1.5 to 1.6 nights, potential earnings from tourism are still unexploited. The town and its surroundings have great scenic and natural resources waiting to be developed.

**Recommendations**

In order to encourage a greater number of visitors and for them to stay longer (and therefore spend more money), the destination must develop a tourist-friendly infrastructure, with innovative attractions and corporate events to draw in the business clientele. We recommend that the Council debate the kind of attractions suitable for the location and the amount of investment it is prepared to make.

### Responding to a complaint

#### Cách viết

Khi trả lời một thư khiếu nại, bạn nên:

- **Cảm ơn** người viết vì đã khiếu nại và vì đã cho bạn biết vấn đề.
- **Xin lỗi** vì trải nghiệm không tốt và **đưa ra lời giải thích**.
- **Nêu rõ hành động hoặc biện pháp** đang được thực hiện hoặc đã thực hiện.
- **Kết thúc trên một nốt tích cực** và đưa ra một đề nghị nào đó.
- Viết **rõ ràng và đơn giản** nhất có thể.
- **Không dùng ngôn ngữ thân mật**, chẳng hạn các dạng viết tắt.
- **Luôn đọc lại thư**, kiểm tra cấu trúc câu, ngữ pháp và chính tả.

> ⚠️ **Hai điều đừng làm.** Thứ nhất, đừng đổ lỗi cho khách hay cho đồng nghiệp — hãy giải thích hoàn cảnh (*owing to changes in personnel*), đừng biện hộ. Thứ hai, đừng thừa nhận trách nhiệm pháp lý bằng những chữ như *we were negligent* hay *it was our fault*; viết *we are investigating your complaint* và *we will take appropriate action if it is considered necessary*.

#### Bài mẫu

**Subject:** Re: The Lakeside Hotel
**Date:** 5 September 2013

Dear Ms Parker

Thank you for your letter of 3 September concerning your visit to the above hotel.

We are sorry to learn about the problems you experienced on these premises, and we are investigating your complaint with the hotel management.

We are passing your comments on to the Registration Officer, who will no doubt take them into consideration when reviewing the hotel's star rating. Comments such as yours are important in our efforts to maintain standards throughout the region.

Although all registered premises are subject to regular inspections, we are sure you will understand that in a service industry such as tourism, standards can vary within a short period of time owing to changes in personnel, management, etc. We will, however, take appropriate action if it is considered necessary.

In conclusion, we hope that your unhappy experience will not prevent you from visiting the region in the future. If we can be of assistance in planning your visit, please do not hesitate to contact us.

Yours sincerely

Leslie O'Gara (Mrs)
Standards Officer

> 📌 **Hai thư xin lỗi để so sánh.** Thư trên là của **cơ quan quản lý** trả lời về một khách sạn thuộc quyền xếp hạng của mình — giọng trung tính, giữ khoảng cách. Thư ở [Review 1 · Bài 11](#bài-11-letter-of-apology) là của **chính khách sạn** — nhận lỗi thẳng, giải thích, và bù bằng voucher. Đọc cả hai và để ý giọng khác nhau ở đâu: *We are sorry to learn* (khoảng cách) so với *I was extremely sorry to learn* (trực tiếp, cá nhân).

## Pairwork Files

> 👥 **Đây là gì:** các thẻ thông tin dành cho hoạt động cặp và nhóm trong lớp (sách giấy tr. 102–112). Mỗi File là phần thông tin mà **một** người được xem — người kia phải hỏi để lấy được. Tên file ghi rõ nó thuộc unit nào, trang nào.

> 🙋 **Học một mình thì dùng thế nào:** ba cách đều có ích. **(1)** Đọc như bài đọc — các thẻ này là văn bản du lịch thật: itinerary, review khách sạn, thống kê, thư khiếu nại, thư luật sư. **(2)** Tự đóng cả hai vai và ghi âm. **(3)** Lấy làm đề viết: đọc thẻ rồi viết lại thành brochure description, report hoặc email theo mẫu ở [Writing Bank](#writing-bank).

> ⚠️ **Lưu ý về bản quét:** mục này in hai đến ba cột trên mỗi trang nên bản PDF quét bị trộn cột nhiều nhất ở đây. Tôi đã tách lại những chỗ tách được; một vài File bị mất số hiệu hoặc lẫn nội dung của File khác — chỗ nào như vậy đều có ghi chú. Thứ tự File cũng không liên tục vì sách xếp xen kẽ để hai bạn ngồi cạnh không đọc được thẻ của nhau.

### File 1 — Unit 1, p.11

**STUDENT B**

The tour operator you work for is creating a new package to the Ang Thong National Marine Park in Thailand. You are about to meet a travel consultant (Student A) who is interested in selling week-long holidays to exotic destinations. Answer the consultant's questions.

#### Ang Thong National Marine Park

The park comprises about 40 islands northwest of Koh Samui, a 45-minute transfer from Bangkok. It covers a total area of 250 square kilometres. Most islands consist of limestone mountains which rise up to 400 meters above sea level. Along the coast there are many, strange-looking caves and dramatic cliffs, dreamy sandy beaches and secluded bays, perfect for relaxation, snorkelling, kayaking and hiking through the rainforest.

**Flights:** London Heathrow – Bangkok-Suvarnabhumi, Thai Airways International, Business Class.

**Days 1–3:** Relax on Koh Samui, in a luxury resort hotel on the southeast coast boasting three gorgeous sandy beaches and beautiful tropical gardens, and offering complete seclusion and relaxation. Visit the island's most popular attraction, the Big Buddha, sitting 15 metres tall. Koh Samui has some of the best nightlife in Thailand, including classical Thai dancing, full moon parties and cabaret shows.

**Day 4:** Take a speedboat tour into the marine park. In the morning, chill out on the beautiful beaches on Koh Wua Talab. In the afternoon, hike with our guide through the interior and enjoy the spectacular views.

**Day 5:** Take a boat to Koh Tao and visit the island by mountain bike.

**Day 6:** Head back into the marine park to visit Koh Mae Ko and go kayaking on Thale Noi, a beautiful saltwater lake surrounded by majestic mountains.

**Day 7:** Spend the day back on Koh Samui and trek around the island on the back of an elephant.

**Price for two:** £5,675, including food, accommodation, local transport and sightseeing.

#### 1 · The climate in Eldorado

The rainy season normally runs from May to November. Flooding is common and widespread. Be aware that during this season (especially in rural areas), landslides, bridge collapses and heavy rains, causing cancellation of local flights, can on occasions affect travel plans.

#### 2 · Tripcouncellor.com — 'The real deal'

This was a terrible experience. They stole more than $300 from my reservation, which the owner refused to return. My wife 'lost' a necklace, which we are sure was taken by a chambermaid. The hotel even asks you to pay more money if you have people visit the hotel lobby. We contacted the police, and even an agency called INTUR which is supposed to represent tourists if they have a complaint. The service was so bad — breakfast is just a cup of coffee with a piece of bread and a little bit of butter. The employees watch every step you take; there is no privacy. They even wait for you at night in order to turn off the hotel lights (including the lobby) and close the gates. In reality, tourists are not welcome here. If you have any complaints, the owner tells you to speak to her lawyer.

#### 3 · Trip Advice

The beach at San Bartoleme del Sur is awful. The water is dirty, brown and polluted. The rubbish rolls in with the waves, and the sea here is definitely not a place to swim.

### File 3 — Unit 2, p.23

> ⚠️ Bản quét mất dòng tiêu đề của File này; nội dung thuộc case study *Improve a service* của Unit 2 (hãng CheapSky).

**GROUP B**

Read and discuss the information below. Use it to give you extra ideas as you plan for the meeting.

Gentlemen: I am writing to demand $1,750 to compensate the death of my pet dwarf rabbit as a result of your airline’s negligence. On April 1, I flew on Flight 195 to Miami to stay with my brother. I have nobody to look after my rabbit so had no choiceher than to take it with me. When I checked in, an agent told me that my rabbit would have to travel in a specially-designed case and placed in the baggage compartment. She became more helpful after I had offered her $100 and finally agreed to let me put my rabbit inside my suitcase.

On arrival, I went to the baggage claim area only to discover that my baggage had not been put on the plane. I was told to fill out a claim form for delayed baggage but when the baggage arrived two days later (with no explanation) and I opened the suitcase, I found to my horror that my rabbit was dead and the inside of the suitcase had been ripped by the rabbit's claws.

I therefore demand $1,500 in reimbursement of my rabbit and the psychological distress, plus the $100 I gave to your agent and $150 for the damage to my suitcase. Sincerely yours,

Anita Pathak

Eagle Airlines has decided that obese people should have to pay for a second seat on their airline or be bumped off the aircraft. The company says that it decided to adopt the tougher policy after receiving more than 700 complaints last year from passengers ‘who did not have a comfortable flight because the person next to them infringed on their seat’, said Eagle

spokeswoman Robin Swift.

There’s a threat of an air-rage rebellion as travellers without children pressure airlines to protect them from their worst travel nightmare — a seat next to a crying or badly-behaved child. Nearly 60 percent of travellers surveyed by price-comparison website Skyscanner said airlines should put all groups with children in a ‘families only’ section on flights, and there have been several recent clashes with parents whose poorly-behaved kids angeredher passengers.

- There should be a friendly guest assistant who greets visitors and finds out if they have a special interest or need. He or she could then advise and direct them accordingly.

- People with access needs should be provided with ramps and handrails; door handles and display cases should be at an appropriate height. For the visually impaired, there should be labels in Braille, large-print guides and, for the deaf, sign-interpreted talks.

- A lift should be installed to enable visitors in a wheelchair to have access to the upper rooms.

It is important to comply with the Equality Act, which states that service providers ‘need to make “reasonable adjustments” to any barriers that put a disabled person at a substantial disadvantage compared to non-disabled people’.

- The path leading to the museum is the responsibility of the local council, not the manor.

- It is not possible to deal with customer comments rapidly as there are not enough staff. We need at least a week, especially if the comments have to be entered into an Excel spreadsheet.

#### Law reports

> ⚠️ Khối này bản quét xếp lẫn vào đây, nhưng nội dung thuộc case study *Improve a service* của Unit 2 (khách quá khổ) — đọc kèm [File 3](#file-3-unit-2-p23).

A civil court has ordered an airline to pay $20,000 in damages to a 27-stone passenger who had his stomach measured at an airport check-in desk before being told to buy two seats. A crew member wrapped him in packing tape to prove that he was too fat for a single seat. In another case, a woman won a lawsuit against Cowperjet when the airline forced her to buy a second seat after she had boarded the plane. In Canada, a law has been passed preventing Canadian airlines from discriminating against clinically obese customers.

### File 5 — Unit 1, p.11

**STUDENT C**

The tour operator you work for is creating a new package to Vanuatu in the South Pacific. You are about to meet a travel consultant (Student A) who is interested in selling week-long holidays to exotic destinations. Answer the consultant's questions.

Vanuatu

Vanuatu is an archipelago of 83 volcanic islands in the South Pacific, about 1,750 kilometres east of northern Australia and just three hours’ flight from Brisbane or Sydney.

Visitor attractions:

Vanuatu is a paradise for those who love diving. The waters surrounding these beautiful islands provide some of the most spectacular underwater exploration in the South Pacific. Experienced divers come from all over the world to explore the many shipwrecks from World War II which lie on the ocean floor. There are tropical rainforests, active volcanoes, sparkling blue lagoons, magnificent coral reef formations, cascading waterfalls and deserted beaches that range from golden yellow to volcanic black.

A week exploring the islands

Days 1-2: Arrive from Port Vila by ferry and settle into your private island resort hotel overlooking the Erakor lagoon. Explore Port Vila, including the colourful daily market.

Day 3: Go scuba-diving, enjoy a barbecue on Tranquility Island (one hour by ferry from Port Vila) and visit a turtle hatchery.

Day 4: Fly to Espiritu Santo and dive down to the wreck of the US President Coolidge which sank in 1942 during the Battle of the Pacific. Explore the fascinating underwater world of tropical fish that live among the magnificent coral formations.

Day 5: Visit Pentecost Island to see the locals take part in the land diving ritual.

Day 6: Fly to Tanna Island and walk up the active Yasur volcano which erupted on 26 May 2009.

Day 7: Return to Port Vila. Visit the Cascade Waterfalls and Ekasup Cultural Village to discover how the Ni-Vanuata people live, including traditional food preparation, herbal medicines, hunting and dancing.

**Price for two:** AUD 3,498, including accommodation, food, local travel and inter-island flights.

### File 7 — Unit 7, p.63

**STUDENT B**

Answer your partner's questions about the Suffragette penny. Then ask your partner questions to complete the information about the Portuguese coins and a jeton.

#### Suffragette penny

This is a British one penny coin which is dated 1903. It shows a profile of King Edward VII but his portrait has been over-stamped with the slogan ‘Votes for Women’. The penny coin was rarely replaced and so this message would be read by many, many people. At this time in history, women were denied the right to vote and considered to be second-class citizens. The suffragettes were women who wanted to be able to take part in political life. Defacing a coin in this way was considered a criminal act and suffragettes could be and were sent to prison for doing so.

#### Portuguese coins and a jeton

Sáu chỗ trống dưới đây là phần Student B phải hỏi Student A mới điền được.

The coins are Portuguese and were made in Lisbon during (4) ______. They might have been lost by the ship's crew. These coins were not used (5) ______ but can often be found in South Wales, and are evidence of extensive trade between the two countries.

Jetons were counters that were used by (6) ______ to help them add and subtract sums of money.

### File 11 — Unit 5, p.46

> ⚠️ Bản quét mất dòng tiêu đề của File này. Nội dung là bốn điểm tham quan ở Pháp, đối ứng với [File 15](#file-15-unit-5-p46) của Student B — thuộc case study *Plan a coach tour* của Unit 5.

**STUDENT A**

**1 The Loire Valley**

- Amboise, Blois, Chambord and Chenonceau are just four of the many spectacular castles in the Loire Valley, mostly dating from the Renaissance era. There are over 100 castles in total.
- Many of the castles have beautifully landscaped ‘French Style’ gardens as impressive as the chateaux themselves.
- Explore troglodyte caves along the banks of the River Loire and visit the vineyards.

**2 Mont Saint Michel**

- Mont Saint Michel is a picturesque fortified village dating back to medieval times on an island off the coast of Normandy. It is a UNESCO World Heritage Site.

- Take the shuttle across to the island at low tide and walk round the numerous boutiques, museums and historic houses.

- Climb to the historic medieval abbey in the centre of the island and enjoy views across the bay.

**3 Chateau de Versailles**

- Just half an hour's drive from Paris (if the traffic allows), this magnificent chateau was built by Louis XIV ‘The Sun King’ during the 17th century.
- It was the official residence of the royal family and 10,000 members of the court.

- Explore the main palace with the spectacular Hall of Mirrors.

- Stroll along the Grand Canal, admire the dozens of fabulous fountains and immense formal gardens.

**4 Caen**

- Take a tour to the nearby Normandy landing beaches and cemeteries (approximately five to six hours).

- Visit the best World War II museum in France, with many artefacts from the French Resistance.
- Watch the video presentations of D-Day from both Allied and German perspectives.

- Take a tour to nearby Bayeux to visit the famous 11th-century Bayeux tapestry, which shows the events leading up to the Norman conquest of Britain.

### File 12 — Unit 7, p.66–67

**GROUP B**

Donostia-San Sebastian Why Donostia-San Sebastian is the best choice

Located in the north of Spain, only 20 km away from the French border, Donostia-San Sebastian is the capital of Gipuzkoa, in the autonomous community of the Basque Country. It is therefore a good example of the co-existence of three distinct cultural influences.

Key events:

Theatre festival in May

Jazz festival in July

Classical music concerts throughout August

Film festival in September (one of the five leading cinema events in Europe)

The Semana Grande with an international fireworks competition, horse-racing, beach volley competition, etc.

The city is important for its celebration of Basque culture and identity: in September there are improvised poetry performances, rowing competitions, stone-lifting contests and traditional games of pelota.

Overall benefits: The aim is to highlight the region's linguistic, cultural and religious diversity and to involve all citizens in ‘the shared construction of a society constantly striving for a future in peace’.

The idea of a Eurocity across the French and Spanish border will be made possible through a new regional transport authority and a high-speed passenger train network.

### File 15 — Unit 5, p.46

**STUDENT B**

**1 Avignon**

- Take a guided tour round the 14th-century Popes’ Palace, the biggest Gothic construction in the world.

- Discover 25 different rooms: reception halls, cloisters, chapels and the Popes’ private apartments and their beautiful wall paintings.

- Take a cruise on the river (famous for its bridge) and visit the Musée Angladon, with its wonderful collection of art by, amonghers, Van Gogh, Picasso and Degas.

**2 Oradour-sur-Glane**

- This village is preserved exactly as the Nazis left it after they had massacred the population during World War II.

- The burnt-out cars and buildings provide a truly emotional experience of the horrors of war.
- Visit the museum to learn about the background to the events leading up to the massacre. There’s also a moving exhibition about the 9/11 terrorist attack in New York.

**3 Beaune**

- Beaune is the capital of Burgundy which offers tours of vineyards and opportunities to learn about viticulture.

- The famous and beautiful Hôtel-Dieu is a hospital built for the poor in the 15th century. Visit the building and the museum, which shows what the hospital was like in medieval times.

- Admire the famous nine-panel painting of The Last Judgment by the Flemish medieval painter Rogier van der Weyden in the Hôtel-Dieu chapel.

**4 Bordeaux**

- This beautiful city is on the banks of the Gironde from which you can visit many nearby chateaux for vineyard tours.

- Learn about the southwest of France at the Museum of Aquitaine, which relates the history of the region, from pre-history to modern times.

- The many fine buildings, the 17th-century cathedral, the Opera House and the Place de la Bourse are well worth visiting.

### File 18 — Unit 7, p.67

**GROUP C**

Manchester

Why Manchester is the best choice

Already a major centre of cultural excellence (the People’s History Museum, the world-class Art Gallery, the Museum of Science and Industry, etc.), Manchester is not a global tourist destination. However, the biannual international festival attracts inventive visual and artistic performances and the city has produced some of the most famous bands in the history of pop music — the Smiths, 10cc, Joy Division, the Happy Mondays, the Chemical Brothers — as well as two legendary football clubs - Manchester City and Manchester United.

Key events: inauguration of the new National Football Museum in the city centre, multi-media performances combining music, technology and nature, an exhibition of original musical instruments, e.g. the sharpsichord and the gravity harp, and Kidstage — theatre productions written and performed by local schools.

Overall benefits: increasing tourism to the north- west of England, establishing a youth arts scheme in the poorer areas of the city, supporting the growth of community arts organizations and local housing projects. The overall aim is one of regeneration — transforming Manchester from a 19th-century city of factories and urban poverty into a 21st-century city of innovation, wealth creation and popular culture.

**STUDENT A**

1 You are planning a trip to visit Beijing. Prepare some questions to help you find out the information below.

- the best time to go
- what to take with you
- how to get there
- how to avoid being ill
- the best place to stay
- advice concerning visas

2 You are a travel agent. Use the information below to advise a client who is planning a trip to visit the Taj Mahal.

Best time to go: February — warm but not too hot. Avoid the summer (April-June) or the rainy season (July-September).

**Getting there:** Choice of car (four-hour journey from Delhi) or plane (30-minute flight to Kharia Airport, 10 km from Agra). N.B. The Taj Mahal is closed on Fridays.

Accommodation: You work with two hotels:

The Hotel Mansingh Palace - good value doubles from €50.

The Oberoi Amarvilas — fantastic views from the rooms — doubles from €350.

Visa: Choose from: 15-day transit visa; 90-day visa; six-month multiple entry visa (phone Consulate for opening times).

Health: Good idea to get vaccinated against tetanus, typhoid and hepatitis. Precautions: mosquitoes — take a course of antimalarial tablets, use insect repellent, keep windows closed at all times, wear shoes and clothes that cover your arms and legs.

Food and drink: Indian food is hot and spicy. Best to choose mild dishes, e.g. chicken biryani. Avoid tap water and eating from roadside and market stalls.

Well worth a visit: Jaipur (hilltop forts, temples, e.g. Govind Dev, historic palaces, e.g. Hawa Mahal — the Palace of Winds).

Practical information: Triple round pin sockets are the norm — best to take an adaptor and a power surge cable (frequent power cuts).

| Điểm ngữ pháp | Unit | Trang sách |

| [Tense review](#tense-review) — các thì hiện tại, quá khứ, tương lai | 1 · 10 | 113 |
